- **aipt-method-adversarial-challenge**: Challenge, objection, counterargument, falsification test, stress-test an assumption, pre-mortem, red-team a proposal, devil's advocate. Write material challenges to a frozen decision packet. Each names the contested premise, the counterevidence or gap, the consequence under the frozen criteria and the resolving test. Keep challenges independent and materially different within a bounded round budget (default two). Use when a consequential proposal rests on uncertain evidence or disputed alternatives. Not for ruling on challenges (aipt-method-evidentiary-adjudication) or applying delegation. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-adversarial-challenge) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-ai-responsibility-allocation**: Responsibility allocation, step ownership, fixed code versus model inference versus adaptive investigation versus human decision, loop budget, termination rule, permission check placement. Use when designing or reviewing a feature or workflow step by step to decide which mechanism is responsible for each step, to justify any adaptive behavior with a named unknown, to keep deterministic permission checks in code, and to bound every loop with a budget and a termination rule. Produces an allocation table as a proposal artifact. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-ai-responsibility-allocation) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-context-files-architecture**: Context files, agent operating files, root guidance, host-specific guidance, specialist role files, command files, skill files, layering, maintainability. Use when a repository or personal workflow needs better operating context for coding assistants, when existing files are vague, stale or bloated, or when universal guidance must be separated from project-specific guidance. Distinguishes file roles, decides what belongs where, keeps files concise and runnable, and proposes a minimal expandable file set. Not for one-off prompt wording or files nobody will maintain. - **aipt-method-controlled-learning**: Controlled learning, skill change proposal, workflow change proposal, repeated failure, verified outcomes, held-out tests, rollback version, protected policy, separate version, anecdote is not a rule. Use when audited results show the same failure recurring across independent cases and a change to a skill, role body or workflow recipe is proposed to address it. Produces a learning proposal on its own version with the failure statement, affected invariants, expected improvement, held-out tests and rollback; blocks any change to protected authority, exclusions, privacy boundaries or thresholds. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-controlled-learning) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-corpus-grounded-onboarding**: Onboarding, handoff absorption, corpus reading, codebase or document-set orientation, mental model, invariants, readiness check. Use when taking over a project, repository, document pack or handoff from a person or another agent and you must understand purpose, workflows, architecture, invariants and gaps before acting. Builds a structured product and system model, separates stated facts from strong inferences and unverified assumptions, marks operational boundaries, and states readiness honestly. Not for quick summaries or when no real corpus exists. - **aipt-method-cost-and-latency-analysis**: Cost per completed task, latency distribution, p95, critical path, retries, dated rates, unknown rates, tokens versus outcomes. Use when a role must state what a feature costs per task that actually completed, or how long users wait end to end, from observed usage, timed traces and dated rate sources. Counts retries, retrieval, tool charges and review time; keeps unknown rates unknown instead of zero; reports latency as a measured end-to-end distribution; never sums parallel stage percentiles; separates critical-path, queued and user-review delay. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-cost-and-latency-analysis) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-delegated-authority**: Delegation, approval, accept a proposal, authority check, who may approve, request human approval, expiry, revocation, version binding, self-authorization. Apply a recorded human delegation to one versioned proposal and return accepted, rejected, deferred or human_required, binding any acceptance to the exact proposal version, hash, scope and expiry. Acceptance is not execution permission; a role cannot grant itself authority; a same-user local approval file is a claim, not proof. Not for ruling on evidence (aipt-method-evidentiary-adjudication) or writing the decision record afterward. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-delegated-authority) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-discovery-interrogation**: Discovery questions, problem reconstruction, decision-critical gaps, consequential questions before design. Use when a request is underspecified, touches workflows, trust, permissions, architecture or operating model, and solving the wrong problem is expensive. Reconstructs the problem from supplied sources, sorts gaps by what they would change, and returns only questions whose answers would change direction, scope, risk handling or success measures. Not for complete specs, trivial gaps, or when the requester has declined questions. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-discovery-interrogation) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-drift-and-regression-audit**: Drift audit, regression check, parity, baseline versus changed, invariants, allowed deltas, hidden drift, migration, refactor, rewrite, consolidation. Compare a baseline version and a changed version of an artifact or behavior against declared invariants and allowed deltas; classify each difference as exact parity, intentional change, ambiguous, regression or hidden drift; root-cause regressions and name the minimum repair or the decision that would bless the deviation. Use after a migration, refactor or rewrite when parity matters. Not for greenfield work with no baseline. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-drift-and-regression-audit) · ★2 · 3 copies)

Synthetic cases are always labeled synthetic. - **aipt-method-evidence-and-lineage**: Evidence records, source scope, claim typing, lineage links, contested claims, impact policy, immutable versions. - **aipt-method-evidentiary-adjudication**: Adjudicate, rule on evidence, judge sufficiency, resolve a disagreement, converge, compare options against criteria, admissible evidence, insufficient evidence, unresolved. - **aipt-method-independent-audit**: Independent audit, verification, frozen artifact, acceptance criteria, re-run checks, pass fail blocked, reviewer independence. - **aipt-method-milestone-planning**: Milestone planning, work orders, execution order, write sets, dependencies, acceptance checks. - **aipt-method-research-consolidation**: Research consolidation, staged research, source inventory, extraction, synthesis, verification, provenance, conflicting evidence, coverage statement. - **aipt-method-status-handoff**: Status handoff, status brief, current state, resume, blockers, next queue, latest validated progress.
